Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electricity object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 821. The moduli space has dimension at most 861 with 4 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the electricity space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 821 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 861. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in electricity theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 486 and the invariant I(A,B) = 874 mod 2.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
